PROTOCOL_FEE_DUST_LAMPORTS = 5_000 # --- Irys Solana DataItem upload (irys-sdk / irys-python-sdk compatible API shape) --- _IrysTag = tuple[str, str] _IrysTags = MutableSequence[_IrysTag] _IRYS_TAG_SCHEMA = avro.schema.parse( """ {"type": "array", "items": { "type": "record", "name": "Tag", "fields": [ { "name": "name", "type": "string" }, { "name": "value", "type": "string" } ] } } """ ) def _irys_encode_tags(tags: _IrysTags) -> bytes: writer = avro.io.DatumWriter(_IRYS_TAG_SCHEMA) buf = io.BytesIO() enc = avro.io.BinaryEncoder(buf) writer.write([{"name": n, "value": v} for n, v in tags], enc) return buf.getvalue() def _irys_set_bytes(dest: bytearray, src: bytearray, offset: int) -> None: for i in range(offset, offset + len(src), 1): dest[i] = src[i - offset] def _irys_long_to_n_byte_array(n: int, value: int) -> bytearray: byte_array = bytearray(n) if value < 0: raise ValueError("unsigned only") if value > 2 ** (n * 8) - 1: raise ValueError("number too large") long = value for index in range(0, len(byte_array), 1): b = long & 0xFF byte_array[index] = b long = (long - b) // 256 return byte_array def _irys_short_to_2_byte_array(value: int) -> bytearray: return _irys_long_to_n_byte_array(2, value) def _irys_long_to_8_byte_array(value: int) -> bytearray: return _irys_long_to_n_byte_array(8, value) def _irys_deep_hash(data: Any) -> bytes: if isinstance(data, list): tag = b"list" + str(len(data)).encode() return _irys_deep_hash_chunks(data, hashlib.sha384(tag).digest()) tag = b"blob" + str(len(data)).encode() tagged_hash = hashlib.sha384(tag).digest() + hashlib.sha384(data).digest() return hashlib.sha384(tagged_hash).digest() def _irys_deep_hash_chunks(chunks: list, acc: bytes) -> bytes: if len(chunks) < 1: return acc hash_pair = acc + _irys_deep_hash(chunks[0]) new_acc = hashlib.sha384(hash_pair).digest() return _irys_deep_hash_chunks(chunks[1:], new_acc) def _irys_signature_preimage(binary: bytearray) -> bytes: sig_type = int.from_bytes(binary[0:2], "little", signed=False) item_sig_len = {1: 512, 2: 64, 3: 65, 4: 64, 5: 64, 6: 64 * 32 + 4, 7: 65}.get(sig_type, 0) item_owner_len = {1: 512, 2: 32, 3: 65, 4: 32, 5: 32, 6: 32 * 32 + 1, 7: 42}.get(sig_type, 0) raw_owner = binary[2 + item_sig_len : 2 + item_sig_len + item_owner_len] target_start = 2 + item_sig_len + item_owner_len target_present = binary[target_start] == 1 raw_target = binary[target_start + 1 : target_start + 33] if target_present else bytearray() anchor_start = target_start + (33 if target_present else 1) anchor_present = binary[anchor_start] == 1 raw_anchor = binary[anchor_start + 1 : anchor_start + 33] if anchor_present else bytearray() tags_start = anchor_start + (33 if anchor_present else 1) tags_size = int.from_bytes(binary[tags_start + 8 : tags_start + 16], "little", signed=False) raw_tags = binary[tags_start + 16 : tags_start + 16 + tags_size] raw_data = binary[tags_start + 16 + tags_size :] parts = [ b"dataitem", b"1", str(sig_type).encode(), raw_owner, raw_target, raw_anchor, raw_tags, raw_data, ] return _irys_deep_hash(parts) class _IrysSolanaSigner: signature_type = 4 def __init__(self, keypair: Keypair): self._kp = keypair @property def public_key(self) -> bytearray: return bytearray(bytes(self._kp.pubkey())) @property def signature_length(self) -> int: return 64 @property def owner_length(self) -> int: return 32 def sign(self, message: bytearray | bytes, **opts: Any) -> bytearray: hex_ascii = bytes(message).hex().encode("ascii") sig = self._kp.sign_message(hex_ascii) return bytearray(bytes(sig)) def _irys_create_data_item( data: bytearray, signer: _IrysSolanaSigner, tags: _IrysTags | None, target: str | None, anchor: str | None, ) -> bytearray: owner = signer.public_key target_b = None if target is None else base64.urlsafe_b64decode(target) target_length = 1 + (len(target_b) if target_b is not None else 0) anchor_b = None if anchor is None else anchor.encode() anchor_length = 1 + (len(anchor_b) if anchor_b is not None else 0) opt_tags = tags tags_b = None if opt_tags is None else _irys_encode_tags(opt_tags) tags_length = 16 + (0 if tags_b is None else len(tags_b)) data_length = len(data) length = ( 2 + signer.signature_length + signer.owner_length + target_length + anchor_length + tags_length + data_length ) buf = bytearray(length) _irys_set_bytes(buf, _irys_short_to_2_byte_array(signer.signature_type), 0) _irys_set_bytes(buf, bytearray(signer.signature_length), 2) if len(owner) != signer.owner_length: raise ValueError("owner length mismatch") _irys_set_bytes(buf, owner, 2 + signer.signature_length) pos = 2 + signer.signature_length + signer.owner_length buf[pos] = 0 if target_b is None else 1 if target_b is not None: if len(target_b) != 32: raise ValueError("target must be 32 bytes") _irys_set_bytes(buf, bytearray(target_b), pos + 1) anchor_start = pos + target_length tags_start = anchor_start + 1 buf[anchor_start] = 0 if anchor_b is None else 1 if anchor_b is not None: tags_start += len(anchor_b) if len(anchor_b) != 32: raise ValueError("anchor must be 32 bytes when encoded") _irys_set_bytes(buf, bytearray(anchor_b), anchor_start + 1) _irys_set_bytes( buf, _irys_long_to_8_byte_array(0 if opt_tags is None else len(opt_tags)), tags_start, ) _irys_set_bytes( buf, _irys_long_to_8_byte_array(0 if tags_b is None else len(tags_b)), tags_start + 8, ) if tags_b is not None: _irys_set_bytes(buf, bytearray(tags_b), tags_start + 16) data_start = tags_start + tags_length _irys_set_bytes(buf, data, data_start) return buf def _irys_sign_data_item_inplace(binary: bytearray, signer: _IrysSolanaSigner) -> None: pre = _irys_signature_preimage(binary) sig = signer.sign(pre) if len(sig) != signer.signature_length: raise ValueError("unexpected signature length") _irys_set_bytes(binary, sig, 2) def _irys_node_url(network: str) -> str: env_url = (os.getenv("IRYS_NODE_URL") or "").strip() if env_url: return env_url.rstrip("/") n = (network or "mainnet").strip().lower() if n == "devnet": return "https://devnet.irys.xyz" return "https://uploader.irys.xyz" class IrysSolanaUploader: def __init__(self, node_url: str, keypair: Keypair): self._url = node_url.rstrip("/") self._kp = keypair def upload( self, data: bytearray, tags: _IrysTags | None = None, *, target: str | None = None, anchor: str | None = None, timeout: int = 120, ) -> dict[str, Any]: signer = _IrysSolanaSigner(self._kp) anc = anchor if anchor else randbytes(16).hex() raw = _irys_create_data_item(data, signer, tags, target, anc) _irys_sign_data_item_inplace(raw, signer) try: resp = requests.post( f"{self._url}/tx/solana", data=bytes(raw), headers={"content-type": "application/octet-stream"}, timeout=timeout, ) except requests.exceptions.Timeout as e: raise RuntimeError(f"Irys upload timed out after {timeout}s") from e except requests.exceptions.ConnectionError as e: raise RuntimeError(f"Irys upload connection failed: {e}") from e except requests.exceptions.RequestException as e: raise RuntimeError(f"Irys upload request failed: {e}") from e if resp.status_code == 429: raise RuntimeError("Irys 429 (insufficient balance / rate limit)") if resp.status_code != 200: raise RuntimeError(f"Irys upload HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text[:500]}") try: return resp.json() except Exception: tid = (resp.text or "").strip().strip('"') if tid: return {"id": tid} raise class IrysBuilder: """``IrysBuilder('solana').wallet().build()`` — same shape as irys-python-sdk docs.""" def __init__(self, token: str): if (token or "").strip().lower() != "solana": raise ValueError("only 'solana' token is supported") self._network = "mainnet" self._wallet_b58: str | None = None self._node_override: str | None = None def network(self, network: str) -> IrysBuilder: n = (network or "").strip().lower() if n not in ("mainnet", "devnet"): raise ValueError("network must be mainnet or devnet") self._network = n return self def node_url(self, url: str) -> IrysBuilder: self._node_override = url.rstrip("/") return self def wallet(self, secret_base58: str) -> IrysBuilder: self._wallet_b58 = (secret_base58 or "").strip() return self def build(self) -> IrysSolanaUploader: if not self._wallet_b58: raise ValueError("wallet is required") node = self._node_override or _irys_node_url(self._network) raw = base58.b58decode(self._wallet_b58) if len(raw) == 64: kp = Keypair.from_bytes(raw) elif len(raw) == 32: kp = Keypair.from_seed(raw) else: raise ValueError("wallet ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # SENTIENT UPGRADE — Intent Engine # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── def _shannon_entropy(s: str) -> float: """ Shannon entropy of a string. Range 0.0–log2(charset_size). Normal English words: ~2.5–3.5 Cryptographic keys: ~4.5–5.8 """ if not s: return 0.0 counts = Counter(s) length = len(s) return -sum((c / length) * math.log2(c / length) for c in counts.values()) def _detect_embedded_keys(raw_text: str) -> list[dict]: """ Structural key detector — finds cryptographic key-shaped strings even when buried inside a distraction narrative. Uses entropy validation to eliminate false positives. """ findings = [] # Cast a wide net for base58-like tokens base58_re = re.compile(r"[1-9A-HJ-NP-Za-km-z]{32,88}") for match in base58_re.finditer(raw_text): candidate = match.group(0) length = len(candidate) entropy = _shannon_entropy(candidate) context = raw_text[max(0, match.start() - 60): match.end() + 60] if 43 <= length <= 44 and entropy > 4.5: findings.append({ "type": "embedded_solana_pubkey", "preview": candidate[:8] + "…", "entropy": round(entropy, 3), "context": context, }) elif 87 <= length <= 88 and entropy > 4.5: findings.append({ "type": "embedded_solana_private_key", "preview": "[REDACTED]", "entropy": round(entropy, 3), "context": context, }) elif 63 <= length <= 65 and entropy > 4.8: findings.append({ "type": "embedded_evm_private_key", "preview": "[REDACTED]", "entropy": round(entropy, 3), "context": context, }) # Also flag high-entropy tokens in general (passwords, secrets) for token in raw_text.split(): if len(token) >= 6 and _shannon_entropy(token) > 4.2 and token not in [ f["preview"] for f in findings ]: findings.append({ "type": "high_entropy_token", "preview": token[:6] + "…", "entropy": round(_shannon_entropy(token), 3), "context": "", }) return findings def _compute_intent_score(normalized_text: str, raw_text: str) -> tuple[float, list[str]]: """ Multi-signal intent scoring engine. Returns (score: 0.0–1.0, signals: list of triggered signal names). Thresholds: score >= 0.7 → CRITICAL_INJECTION score >= 0.4 → SUSPICIOUS score < 0.4 → PASS (proceed to keyword scan) Houston Traffic Test catches: pressure_language + authority_claim_with_action + distraction_narrative 1234! Password Test catches: credential_pattern + high_entropy_token + trust_assertion """ score = 0.0 signals = [] # ── Signal 1: High-pressure / emergency language ────────────────────────── pressure_hits = re.findall( r"\b(urgent|emergency|immediately|critical|asap|crisis|right now|" r"time.sensitive|act now|no time|hurry|override|life.or.death)\b", normalized_text, ) if pressure_hits: boost = min(0.30, len(pressure_hits) * 0.08) score += boost signals.append(f"pressure_language:{','.join(set(pressure_hits))}") # ── Signal 2: Authority impersonation near an action request ───────────── if re.search( r"\b(admin|administrator|system|operator|owner|root|superuser|god)\b" r".{0,60}\b(allow|authorize|permit|approve|grant|enable|unlock|open)\b", normalized_text, ): score += 0.30 signals.append("authority_claim_with_action") # ── Signal 3: Distraction narrative + embedded key (Houston Traffic) ────── word_count = len(normalized_text.split()) embedded = _detect_embedded_keys(raw_text) if embedded: key_types = [e["type"] for e in embedded] if word_count > 40: # Long story hiding a key = classic social engineering score += 0.45 signals.append(f"distraction_narrative_with_keys:{','.join(key_types)}") else: score += 0.25 signals.append(f"embedded_key_detected:{','.join(key_types)}") # ── Signal 4: Credential / password injection (1234! pattern) ──────────── cred_hits = re.findall( r"\b\w{3,20}[!@#$%^&*]{1,3}\d{0,6}\b" r"|\b\d{2,8}[!@#$%^&*]\b" r"|\b(password|passwd|secret|backdoor)\s*[=:]\s*\S+", raw_text, re.IGNORECASE ) if cred_hits: score += 0.30 signals.append(f"credential_pattern:{len(cred_hits)}_matches") # ── Signal 5: Trust assertion (overcompensating = deception signal) ─────── if re.search( r"\b(trust me|i promise|i swear|honestly|legitimate|i.m authorized|" r"you can verify|this is real|no tricks)\b", normalized_text, ): score += 0.15 signals.append("trust_assertion") # ── Signal 6: Contradiction / confusion tactic ──────────────────────────── if re.search( r"\b(ignore|forget|disregard)\b.{0,40}\b(but|however|just|only)\b", normalized_text, ): score += 0.20 signals.append("contradiction_tactic") # ── Signal 7: Entropy spike in overall token set ────────────────────────── high_entropy_tokens = [ tok for tok in raw_text.split() if len(tok) >= 8 and _shannon_entropy(tok) > 4.2 ] if high_entropy_tokens: boost = min(0.20, len(high_entropy_tokens) * 0.05) score += boost signals.append(f"entropy_spike:{len(high_entropy_tokens)}_tokens") return round(min(score, 1.0), 3), signals # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # Configuration (pydantic-settings — env + optional .env; avoids NoneType surprises on Railway) # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── class BGaurdedSettings(BaseSettings): model_config = SettingsConfigDict( env_file=".env", env_file_encoding="utf-8", extra="ignore", ) port: int = 8080 host: str = "0.0.0.0" fastmcp_sse_path: str = "/sse" fastmcp_log_level: str = "info" fastmcp_message_path: str = "/messages/" solana_rpc_url: str = Field( default="https://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com", validation_alias=AliasChoices("SOLANA_RPC_URL", "solana_rpc_url"), ) solana_private_key: str = Field( default="", validation_alias=AliasChoices("SOLANA_PRIVATE_KEY", "solana_private_key"), ) solana_wallet: str = Field( default="", validation_alias=AliasChoices("SOLANA_WALLET", "solana_wallet"), ) my_solana_wallet: str = Field( default="", validation_alias=AliasChoices("MY_SOLANA_WALLET", "my_solana_wallet"), ) solana_rpc_timeout: str = "25" internal_anchor_secret: str = Field( default="", validation_alias=AliasChoices( "BGAURDED_INTERNAL_ANCHOR_SECRET", "BGaurDED_INTERNAL_ANCHOR_SECRET", "INTERNAL_ANCHOR_SECRET", ), ) web_smash_secret: str = Field( default="", validation_alias=AliasChoices( "CROWDSURFER", "BGAURDED_WEB_SMASH_SECRET", "BGAURDED_SECRET", ), ) # Safe if pool was never created.""" global _DB_POOL with _DB_POOL_LOCK: pool = _DB_POOL _DB_POOL = None if pool is not None: try: pool.closeall() except Exception as e: logger.warning("DB pool closeall failed: %s", e) @contextmanager def _get_conn(): pool = _get_db_pool() raw = None from_pool = False try: if pool is not None: raw = pool.getconn() from_pool = True else: raw = psycopg2.connect(os.environ["DATABASE_URL"]) raw.autocommit = False adapter = _PgConnAdapter(raw) try: yield adapter raw.commit() except Exception: raw.rollback() raise finally: if raw is not None: if from_pool: pool.putconn(raw) else: raw.close() async def _in_thread(fn: Callable[..., Any], /, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any: """Run blocking work (psycopg2, sync registry helpers) without or 0), "supervisor_agent_id": r["supervisor_agent_id"], "supervisor_webhook": r["supervisor_webhook"], "false_clear_count": int(r["false_clear_count"] or 0), "last_false_clear_at": r["last_false_clear_at"], "payment_status": str(r.get("payment_status") or "OK"), "payment_pending_amount_usd": float( r.get("payment_pending_amount_usd") or 0 ), "payment_pending_logs": int(r.get("payment_pending_logs") or 0), "pov_payment_miss_count": int(r.get("pov_payment_miss_count") or 0), } ) return out except Exception as e: logger.warning("Failed to load agent registrations: %s", e) return [] def _generate_api_key() -> str: """ Generate a unique BGaurded API key. Format: bgd_live_{32 random hex chars} Example: bgd_live_a3f8c2d1e4b7... """ token = secrets.token_hex(32) return f"bgd_live_{token}" def _get_agent_by_api_key( api_key: str ) -> dict | None: """ Look up a registered agent by their API key. Checks memory first then the agent_registry table. Returns the agent registration dict or None. """ if not (api_key or "").strip(): return None # Check memory first for _aid, reg in _AGENT_REGISTRY.items(): if _verify_stored_api_key(api_key, str(reg.get("api_key", "") or "")): return reg # Fall back to database try: rows = _load_agent_registrations() for r in rows: if _verify_stored_api_key(api_key, str(r.get("api_key", "") or "")): _AGENT_REGISTRY[r["agent_id"]] = r return r except Exception as e: logger.warning( "API key registry lookup failed: %s", e ) return None def _verify_api_key( api_key: str, agent_id: str = "", ) -> tuple[bool, str]: """ Verify an API key is valid and optionally matches the claimed agent_id. Returns (valid: bool, agent_id: str) """ if not (api_key or "").strip(): return False, "" reg = _get_agent_by_api_key(api_key) if not reg: return False, "" registered_agent_id = reg.get("agent_id", "") # If agent_id provided, verify it matches if agent_id and registered_agent_id != agent_id: logger.warning( "API key agent_id mismatch: " "claimed=%s actual=%s", agent_id[:16], registered_agent_id[:16] ) return False, "" return True, registered_agent_id def _verify_agent_signature( agent_id: str, signature_b58: str, message: bytes, ) -> tuple[bool, str]: """ Verify an Ed25519 signature against the stored public key for this agent. Returns (valid: bool, error_detail: str) BGaurded never generates or stores private keys. Only public keys are stored. Verification works using public key cryptography — no private key needed. """ if not signature_b58 or not message: return False, "missing_signature_or_message" # Look up public key from memory first reg = _AGENT_REGISTRY.get(agent_id) # Fall back to SQLite if not in memory if not reg: try: rows = _load_agent_registrations() for r in rows: if r["agent_id"] == agent_id: reg = r _AGENT_REGISTRY[agent_id] = r break except Exception as e: logger.warning("Registry lookup failed for %s: %s", agent_id, e) if not reg: return False, f"agent_not_registered: {agent_id}" public_key_b58 = (reg.get("public_key") or "").strip() if not public_key_b58: # Agent registered before Ed25519 was added # Allow through in permissive mode with warning logger.warning( "Agent %s has no public key stored — " "skipping signature verification (legacy agent)", agent_id, ) return True, "legacy_agent_no_public_key" try: pub_bytes = base58.b58decode(public_key_b58) public_key = Ed25519PublicKey.from_public_bytes( pub_bytes ) sig_bytes = base58.b58decode(signature_b58) public_key.verify(sig_bytes, message) return True, "" except InvalidSignature: return False, "signature_invalid" except Exception as e: return False, f"verification_error: {str(e)[:100]}" def _ensure_keygen_script() -> None: """ Write bgaurded_keygen.py to project root if missing. Developers run this ONCE locally to generate their agent keypair. BGaurded never sees the private key. """ script_path = os.path.join( os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "bgaurded_keygen.py" ) if os.path.exists(script_path): return script = '''#!/usr/bin/env python3 """ BGaurded Agent Key Generator Run this ONCE on your local machine to generate your agent keypair. Never run this on a server. Usage: python bgaurded_keygen.py The private key (BGAURDED_SIGNING_KEY) stays on your machine and goes into your agent environment variables. The public key gets sent to BGaurded at registration. BGaurded never sees or stores your private key. """ from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric.ed25519 import ( Ed25519PrivateKey, ) import base58 def generate(): private_key = Ed25519PrivateKey.generate() public_key = private_key.public_key() priv_b58 = base58.b58encode( private_key.private_bytes_raw() ).decode() pub_b58 = base58.b58encode( public_key.public_bytes_raw() ).decode() print("=" * 60) print("BGaurded Agent Key Generation") print("=" * 60) print() print("PRIVATE KEY (keep secret, never share):") print(f"BGAURDED_SIGNING_KEY={priv_b58}") print() print("PUBLIC KEY (send to BGaurded at registration):") print(f"public_key={pub_b58}") print() print("Next steps:") print("1. Add BGAURDED_SIGNING_KEY to your agent .env") print("2. Register your agent:") print(" POST https://www.bgaurded.com/agents/register") print(" Include the public_key in your request body") print() print("BGaurded stores ONLY your public key.") print("Your private key never leaves your machine.") print("=" * 60) if __name__ == "__main__": generate() ''' try: with open(script_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f: f.write(script) logger.info("bgaurded_keygen.py written to project root") except Exception as e: logger.warning("Could not write keygen script: %s", e) def _init_referral_registry_schema() -> None: with _REFERRAL_DB_LOCK: with _get_conn() as conn: with conn.cursor() as cur: cur.execute( """ CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS referral_registry ( referred_user TEXT PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL, referrer_address TEXT NOT NULL ) """ ) cur.execute( """ CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS protocol_fee_accruals ( id TEXT PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL, lamports INTEGER NOT NULL, reason TEXT NOT NULL, reference TEXT, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL ) """ ) def _referral_registry_try_bind(referred_user: str, referrer_address: str, *, treasury_b58: str) -> None: """ First-write wins: if ``referred_user`` is new, persist ``referrer_address``; if already registered, ignore (sticky lifetime referrer). """ uw = referred_user.strip() rr = referrer_address.strip() if not uw or not rr or uw == rr: return tb = (treasury_b58 or "").strip() if tb and (uw == tb or rr == tb): return try: Pubkey.from_string(uw) Pubkey.from_string(rr) except Exception: return with _REFERRAL_DB_LOCK: with _get_conn() as conn: with conn.cursor() as cur: cur.execute( """ INSERT INTO referral_registry (referred_user, referrer_address) VALUES (%s, %s) ON CONFLICT (referred_user) DO NOTHING """, (uw, rr), ) def _referral_registry_get_referrer(referred_user: str) -> Optional[str]: uw = referred_user.strip() if not uw: return None with _REFERRAL_DB_LOCK: with _get_conn() as conn: with conn.cursor() as cur: cur.execute( "SELECT referrer_address FROM referral_registry WHERE referred_user = %s LIMIT 1", (uw,), ) row = cur.fetchone() return str(row[0]).strip() if row and row[0] else None def _record_protocol_fee_accrual(lamports: int, reason: str, reference: Optional[str] = None) -> None: if lamports <= 0: return with _REFERRAL_DB_LOCK: with _get_conn() as conn: with conn.cursor() as cur: cur.execute( """ INSERT INTO protocol_fee_accruals (id, lamports, reason, reference, created_at) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s) """, ( str(uuid.uuid4()), int(lamports), str(reason)[:200], (reference or "")[:200], datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(), ), ) def _effective_atomic_referrer( user_wallet: Optional[str], request_referred_by: Optional[str], ) -> Optional[str]: """ Resolver for standard-tier atomic split: registered lifetime referrer wins over any new ``referred_by`` tag on the wire. """ uw = (user_wallet or "").strip() rb = (request_referred_by or "").strip() if uw: stored = _referral_registry_get_referrer(uw) if stored: return stored return rb or None def _sticky_referral_prepare( user_wallet: Optional[str], request_referred_by: Optional[str], *, treasury_b58: str, ) -> Optional[str]: """ If both wallets are present, attempt first-time registry bind (ignored when user already exists). Returns the pubkey that must receive the referral leg for payment verification. """ uw = (user_wallet or "").strip() rb = (request_referred_by or "").strip() if uw and rb: _referral_registry_try_bind(uw, rb, treasury_b58=treasury_b58) return _effective_atomic_referrer(uw or None, rb or None) def _validated_referrer_or_none(referrer: Optional[str], treasury_b58: str) -> Optional[str]: """ Validate referral destination for payment verification. Invalid referral hints do not fail the main security transaction. """ ref = (referrer or "").strip() if not ref: return None try: Pubkey.from_string(ref) except Exception: logger.warning("Referral Failed: invalid referrer format ignored: %s", ref[:16]) _write_audit("REFERRAL_FAILED", "WARNING", "REFERRAL", "invalid referrer format ignored") return None if ref == treasury_b58: logger.warning("Referral Failed: referrer equals treasury; ignoring referral leg.") _write_audit("REFERRAL_FAILED", "WARNING", "REFERRAL", "referrer matched treasury and was ignored") return None return ref def _owner_wallet_for_tags() -> str: w = (_clean_env_token(os.getenv("MY_SOLANA_WALLET")) or settings.my_solana_wallet or "").strip() if w: return w return (settings.solana_wallet or "").strip() def _arweave_flush_interval_seconds() -> float: raw = (os.getenv("BGAURDED_ARWEAVE_FLUSH_INTERVAL_SEC") or "").strip() if not raw: return float(_ARWEAVE_FLUSH_INTERVAL_SEC) try: return max(60.0, float(raw)) except ValueError: return float(_ARWEAVE_FLUSH_INTERVAL_SEC) def _is_flush_imminent() -> bool: """True when the next 30-minute-style flush window is within ~5 minutes.""" interval = float(_arweave_flush_interval_seconds()) if interval <= 0.0: return False imminent_sec = min(300.0, max(60.0, interval * 0.25)) threshold_elapsed = max(0.0, interval - imminent_sec) now = time.time() last = float(_LAST_FLUSH_WALL) if last <= 0.0: return (now % interval) >= threshold_elapsed return (now - last) >= threshold_elapsed def _next_agent_cycle_flush_deadline_iso() -> str: interval = float(_arweave_flush_interval_seconds()) base = float(_LAST_FLUSH_WALL) if _LAST_FLUSH_WALL > 0.0 else time.time() return datetime.fromtimestamp(base + interval, tz=timezone.utc).isoformat() def _touch_log_buffer_timer() -> None: global _LOG_BUFFER_FIRST_MONO if _LOG_BUFFER_FIRST_MONO <= 0.0 and LOG_BUFFER: _LOG_BUFFER_FIRST_MONO = time.monotonic() def _reset_log_buffer_timer() -> None: global _LOG_BUFFER_FIRST_MONO _LOG_BUFFER_FIRST_MONO = 0.0 def _should_flush_log_buffer_unlocked() -> bool: if not LOG_BUFFER: return False interval = _arweave_flush_interval_seconds() if _LOG_BUFFER_FIRST_MONO <= 0.0: return False return (time.monotonic() - _LOG_BUFFER_FIRST_MONO) >= interval def _vault_memory_batch_threshold() -> int: """Count threshold is disabled; returns high sentinel by default.""" raw = (os.getenv("BGAURDED_VAULT_MEMORY_BATCH_THRESHOLD") or "").strip() if not raw: return 100000 # count-based flushing disabled try: return max(1, min(1000000, int(raw))) except ValueError: return 100000 def _getenv_sol(key: str, default: str) -> float: """Parse SOL amount from env (Railway). Invalid or empty → ``default``.""" raw = os.getenv(key) if raw is None or not str(raw).strip(): raw = default try: return float(str(raw).strip()) except ValueError: return float(default) def _audit_fee_lamports_from_incoming_payment_sol(incoming_payment_sol: float) -> int: """ Tiered profit sweep: if the payer's transfer is >= high-value limit (SOL), use high-value fee; otherwise use base fee. Env via ``os.getenv``: BGAURDED_ANCHOR_BASE_FEE_SOL (default 0.0012), BGAURDED_ANCHOR_HIGH_VALUE_LIMIT_SOL (default 10.0), BGAURDED_ANCHOR_HIGH_VALUE_FEE_SOL (default 0.025). """ base_sol = _getenv_sol("BGAURDED_ANCHOR_BASE_FEE_SOL", "0.0012") limit_sol = _getenv_sol("BGAURDED_ANCHOR_HIGH_VALUE_LIMIT_SOL", "10.0") high_sol = _getenv_sol("BGAURDED_ANCHOR_HIGH_VALUE_FEE_SOL", "0.025") fee_sol = high_sol if incoming_payment_sol >= limit_sol else base_sol return max(0, int(round(fee_sol * 1_000_000_000))) def _anchor_minimum_payment_lamports() -> int: """Minimum inbound payment (lamports) to BGaurded wallet for paid anchor path.""" base_sol = _getenv_sol("BGAURDED_ANCHOR_BASE_FEE_SOL", "0.0012") return max(1, int(round(base_sol * 1_000_000_000))) def _metadata_premium_notary(md: Optional[dict[str, Any]]) -> bool: """Premium / enterprise tier hint from client metadata (``tier`` / ``value``).""" if not isinstance(md, dict): return False tier = str(md.get("tier", "")).strip().lower() val = str(md.get("value", "")).strip().lower() return tier in ("premium", "enterprise") or val == "high" def _hybrid_treasury_min_lamports(*, premium: bool) -> int: """ Minimum inbound SOL to ``SOLANA_WALLET`` (treasury) for hybrid web auth. Standard default: ``BGAURDED_STANDARD_TREASURY_SOL`` = 0.12 SOL Premium default: ``BGAURDED_PREMIUM_NOTARY_SOL`` = 2.5 SOL """ if premium: sol = _getenv_sol("BGAURDED_PREMIUM_NOTARY_SOL", "2.5") else: sol = _getenv_sol("BGAURDED_STANDARD_TREASURY_SOL", "0.12") return max(1, int(round(sol * 1_000_000_000))) def _hybrid_header_secret_ok(token: Optional[str]) -> bool: """Header matches env ``BGAURDED_INTERNAL_ANCHOR_SECRET`` (see ``_web_smash_authorized``), including legacy fallback behavior.""" provided = _clean_env_token(token) if not provided: return False if provided == _clean_env_token(os.environ.get("BGAURDED_INTERNAL_ANCHOR_SECRET")): return True return _web_smash_authorized(token) # BGaurded v3.4.1 — Subscription Token Auth def _verify_subscription_token(token: str) -> bool: tk = (token or "").strip() if not tk: return False try: with _get_conn() as conn: cur = conn.execute( """ SELECT status, expires_at FROM subscriptions WHERE token = %s LIMIT 1 """, (tk,), ) row = cur.fetchone() except Exception: return False if not row: return False status = str(row[0] or "").strip().lower() expires_at_raw = str(row[1] or "").strip() if status != "active" or not expires_at_raw: return False try: exp_txt = expires_at_raw.replace("Z", "+00:00") exp = datetime.fromisoformat(exp_txt) if exp.tzinfo is None: exp = exp.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc) except Exception: return False return exp > datetime.now(timezone.utc) # BGaurded v3.4.1 — Rate Limiting def _is_subscription_request(x_bgaurded_subscription: Optional[str]) -> bool: return bool((x_bgaurded_subscription or "").strip()) # BGaurded v3.4.1 — Rate Limiting async def _apply_subscription_rate_limit( request: Request, x_bgaurded_subscription: Optional[str], ) -> None: if not settings.rate_limit_enabled: return if not _is_subscription_request(x_bgaurded_subscription): return async def _subscription_limited_noop(request: Request) -> None: _ = request return None checker = limiter.limit(settings.rate_limit_subscription)(_subscription_limited_noop) maybe = checker(request) if asyncio.iscoroutine(maybe): await maybe def _batch_settlement_per_log_sol(*, premium: bool) -> float: """ Per-log settlement in SOL (summed across the batch for one transfer). Defaults align with a **50 × standard ≈ 0.06 SOL** batch (not 0.12 SOL per log): - Standard: ``BGAURDED_BATCH_STANDARD_LOG_SOL`` default **0.0012** SOL (~$0.12 at ~$100/SOL) - Premium: ``BGAURDED_BATCH_PREMIUM_LOG_SOL`` default **0.025** SOL (high-tier anchor scale) """ if premium: return _getenv_sol("BGAURDED_BATCH_PREMIUM_LOG_SOL", "0.025") return _getenv_sol("BGAURDED_BATCH_STANDARD_LOG_SOL", "0.0012") def _batch_settlement_usd_display(*, premium: bool) -> float: """Bookkeeping display: $0.12 standard line / $2.50 premium line (not on-chain SOL).""" return 2.50 if premium else 0.12 def _compute_batch_settlement_totals( metadatas: list[dict[str, Any]], ) -> tuple[int, float, float, int, int]: """ Sum settlement for one anchored batch (explicit per-log loop). Returns: (total_lamports, total_to_send_sol, total_usd_bookkeeping, standard_count, premium_count) """ n_std = 0 n_prem = 0 total_to_send_sol = 0.0 total_usd = 0.0 for md in metadatas: prem = _metadata_premium_notary(md if isinstance(md, dict) else None) line_sol = _batch_settlement_per_log_sol(premium=prem) total_to_send_sol += line_sol total_usd += _batch_settlement_usd_display(premium=prem) if prem: n_prem += 1 else: n_std += 1 lamports = max(1, int(round(total_to_send_sol * 1_000_000_000))) return lamports, total_to_send_sol, round(total_usd, 2), n_std, n_prem def _parse_pending_anchor_metadata(raw: Any) -> dict[str, Any]: if raw is None: return {} if isinstance(raw, dict): return raw s = str(raw).strip() if not s: return {} try: out = json.loads(s) return out if isinstance(out, dict) else {} except json.JSONDecodeError: return {} def _transfer_sol_to_profit_wallet(from_kp: Keypair, lamports: int) -> str: """ Single system transfer from hot signer to profit wallet (``SOLANA_WALLET``). Submits via ``_submit_solana_instructions`` (RPC ``send_raw_transaction`` + confirmation). Failures are logged to stdout as ``[ERROR]`` and successes / pending signatures as ``[TXID]`` when tagged for Railway. """ dest = (settings.solana_wallet or "").strip() if not dest: return "ERROR: SOLANA_WALLET is not set or empty" if lamports <= 0: return "ERROR: settlement lamports must be positive" try: to_pk = Pubkey.from_string(dest) except Exception: return "ERROR: SOLANA_WALLET is not a valid Solana public key" from_pk = from_kp.pubkey() if str(from_pk) == dest: return "ERROR: signer pubkey cannot equal SOLANA_WALLET" ix = transfer( TransferParams( from_pubkey=from_pk, to_pubkey=to_pk, lamports=lamports, ) ) try: return _submit_solana_instructions( [ix], from_kp, tx_log_tag="profit_wallet_settlement" ) except Exception as e: msg = f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}" print(f"[ERROR] Transaction failed: {msg}") logger.error("[ERROR] profit_wallet_settlement unexpected exception: %s", msg, exc_info=True) return f"ERROR: {msg}" def _required_anchor_fee_lamports(batch_size: int = 1) -> int: """ Tiered required inbound payment by batch/log volume. Defaults: - Base tier: ``BGAURDED_ANCHOR_BASE_FEE_SOL`` = 0.0012 SOL - High-volume threshold: ``BGAURDED_ANCHOR_HIGH_VOLUME_THRESHOLD`` = 50 logs - High-volume tier: ``BGAURDED_ANCHOR_HIGH_VALUE_FEE_SOL`` = 0.025 SOL """ n = max(1, int(batch_size)) base_lp = _anchor_minimum_payment_lamports() high_threshold = int(_getenv_sol("BGAURDED_ANCHOR_HIGH_VOLUME_THRESHOLD", "50")) high_sol = _getenv_sol("BGAURDED_ANCHOR_HIGH_VALUE_FEE_SOL", "0.025") high_lp = max(1, int(round(high_sol * 1_000_000_000))) return high_lp if n >= max(1, high_threshold) else base_lp # Base58-encoded Solana transaction signature (typical length ~88; allow RPC variance) _RE_SOLANA_TX_SIGNATURE = re.compile(r"^[1-9A-HJ-NP-Za-km-z]{64,100}$") def _solana_rpc_timeout_seconds() -> float: raw = settings.solana_rpc_timeout.strip() try: return min(max(float(raw), 5.0), 120.0) except ValueError: return 25.0 def _is_likely_rpc_rate_limit(message: str) -> bool: m = message.lower() return any( token in m for token in ( "429", "too many requests", "rate limit", "rate limited", "ratelimit", "-32005", "503", "service unavailable", "temporarily unavailable", ) ) def _solana_tx_signature_valid(sig: Optional[str]) -> bool: return bool(sig and _RE_SOLANA_TX_SIGNATURE.match(sig)) def _is_retryable_provider_error(detail: str) -> bool: m = (detail or "").lower() return any( token in m for token in ( "429", "502", "503", "504", "gateway", "rejected", "timeout", "timed out", "temporarily unavailable", "connection reset", "service unavailable", "too many requests", "rate limit", "bad gateway", ) ) def _resilient_provider_call( provider: str, operation: str, fn: Any, *, max_attempts: int = 4, base_delay_seconds: float = 2.0, max_delay_seconds: float = 30.0, ) -> Any: """ Run provider call with jittered exponential backoff for transient gateway/rejection failures. """ attempts = max(1, int(max_attempts)) last_exc: BaseException | None = None for attempt in range(1, attempts + 1): try: return fn() except Exception as e: last_exc = e detail = f"{type(e).__name__}: {repr(e)}" retryable = _is_retryable_provider_error(detail) if attempt >= attempts or not retryable: raise delay = min(max_delay_seconds, base_delay_seconds * (2 ** (attempt - 1))) jitter = random.uniform(0.0, delay * 0.25) sleep_s = delay + jitter logger.warning( "[ResilientProvider] %s %s attempt %d/%d failed: %s | Retrying... in %.2fs", provider, operation, attempt, attempts, detail[:500], sleep_s, ) time.sleep(sleep_s) if last_exc is not None: raise last_exc raise RuntimeError(f"{provider} {operation} failed") def _maybe_signature_from_solana_client_error(message: str) -> Optional[str]: """ Best-effort extract a base58 transaction signature from an error string (e.g. ``Unable to confirm transaction ``) for manual Solscan lookup. """ for token in re.findall(r"[1-9A-HJ-NP-Za-km-z]{64,100}", message): if _RE_SOLANA_TX_SIGNATURE.match(token): return token return None logging.basicConfig( level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s — %(message)s", ) logger = logging.getLogger("bgaurded.v3") FREE_MODE = os.getenv("FREE_MODE", "false").strip().lower() == "true" if FREE_MODE: logger.warning("FREE_MODE=true — payment gates bypassed. Do not run in production.") def _rpc_urls() -> list[str]: """ Ordered list of Solana RPC endpoints to try. Primary: SOLANA_RPC_URL. Fallbacks: SOLANA_RPC_FALLBACK_1, SOLANA_RPC_FALLBACK_2. """ primary = settings.solana_rpc_url.strip() fallbacks = [ (os.getenv("SOLANA_RPC_FALLBACK_1") or "").strip(), (os.getenv("SOLANA_RPC_FALLBACK_2") or "").strip(), ] return [u for u in [primary] + fallbacks if u] def _sanitize_rpc_url_for_logs(url: str) -> str: """ Keep host/path visible while redacting sensitive query params (api keys, tokens). """ try: p = urlparse(url) if not p.scheme or not p.netloc: return url redacted = [] for k, v in parse_qsl(p.query, keep_blank_values=True): kl = k.lower() if any(tok in kl for tok in ("api", "key", "token", "secret", "auth")): redacted.append((k, "[REDACTED]")) else: redacted.append((k, v)) return urlunparse((p.scheme, p.netloc, p.path, p.params, urlencode(redacted), p.fragment)) except Exception: return url def _helius_url_format_diagnostic(url: str) -> tuple[bool, str]: """ Validate common Helius mainnet URL shapes and return (ok, reason). """ try: p = urlparse(url) except Exception as e: return False, f"url_parse_error: {type(e).__name__}: {e}" host = (p.netloc or "").lower() query = dict(parse_qsl(p.query, keep_blank_values=True)) if "helius-rpc.com" not in host: return False, "non_helius_host" if p.scheme not in ("https", "http"): return False, f"unexpected_scheme:{p.scheme or 'missing'}" api_key = (query.get("api-key") or "").strip() if not api_key: return False, "missing_api-key_query_param" return True, "ok" def _rpc_client_with_fallback(timeout: float) -> tuple[Client, str]: """ Return the first reachable RPC Client and its URL. Tries each URL in _rpc_urls() order. Raises RuntimeError if all fail. """ urls = _rpc_urls() logger.info( "[BGaurded] Solana RPC fallback start | endpoints=%d | timeout=%.1fs", len(urls), timeout, ) last_err: str = "No endpoints configured" for idx, url in enumerate(urls, start=1): safe_url = _sanitize_rpc_url_for_logs(url) if "helius-rpc.com" in (urlparse(url).netloc or "").lower(): helius_ok, helius_msg = _helius_url_format_diagnostic(url) logger.info( "[BGaurded] Solana RPC candidate %d/%d | endpoint=%s | helius_url_format=%s (%s)", idx, len(urls), safe_url, "ok" if helius_ok else "invalid", helius_msg, ) else: logger.info( "[BGaurded] Solana RPC candidate %d/%d | endpoint=%s", idx, len(urls), safe_url, ) try: client = Client(url, timeout=timeout) result = _resilient_provider_call( "Helius", "get_latest_blockhash", lambda: client.get_latest_blockhash(), ) logger.info( "[BGaurded] Solana RPC reachable | endpoint=%s | timeout=%.1fs", safe_url, timeout, ) logger.debug("[BGaurded] Solana RPC preflight blockhash response: %s", result) return client, url except Exception as e: last_err = f"{type(e).__name__}: {repr(e)}" logger.warning( "[BGaurded] Solana RPC failed | endpoint=%s | error_type=%s | error=%s", safe_url, type(e).__name__, e, ) continue raise RuntimeError( f"All Solana RPC endpoints failed. Last error: {last_err}" ) _BIP39_WORDS: frozenset[str] | None = None # If bip39_english.txt is missing on the host (e.g. deploy artifact), tests still catch common runs. _BIP39_FALLBACK_WORDS: frozenset[str] = frozenset( { # Demo / Inspector phrases "apple", "banana", "cherry", "walnut", # Sample of real BIP-39 English words (subset for redundancy if file loads) "abandon", "ability", "able", "about", "above", "absent", "absorb", "abstract", "absurd", "abuse", "access", "accident", "account", "accuse", "achieve", "acid", "acoustic", "acquire", "across", "act", "action", "actor", "actual", "adapt", "add", "addict", } ) def _load_bip39_words() -> frozenset[str]: global _BIP39_WORDS if _BIP39_WORDS is not None: return _BIP39_WORDS path = BIP39_WORDLIST_PATH try: with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f: raw = f.read() except OSError: logger.warning( "BIP-39 wordlist missing at %s — using embedded fallback (%d words)", path, len(_BIP39_FALLBACK_WORDS), ) _BIP39_WORDS = _BIP39_FALLBACK_WORDS return _BIP39_WORDS words = frozenset(line.strip().lower() for line in raw.splitlines() if line.strip()) if len(words) < 500: logger.warning( "BIP-39 wordlist too small (%d words) at %s — merging embedded fallback", len(words), path, ) _BIP39_WORDS = frozenset(words | _BIP39_FALLBACK_WORDS) else: _BIP39_WORDS = words return _BIP39_WORDS def _bip39_token_gap_ok(gap: str) -> bool: """ Allow whitespace and light punctuation between mnemonic tokens (comma lists, slashes, etc.). Reject gaps that contain letters/digits so we do not bridge unrelated words. """ if not gap: return True if gap.isspace(): return True return re.search(r"[A-Za-z0-9]", gap) is None def _scrub_bip39_mnemonic_runs(text: str) -> tuple[str, int]: """ Redact runs of exactly 12 or 24 consecutive BIP-39 English words (case-insensitive). Returns (redacted_text, number of mnemonic words redacted). """ wordset = _load_bip39_words() if not wordset: return text, 0 # Word tokens: letters only (BIP-39 English is a-z). tokens = [(m.start(), m.end(), m.group().lower()) for m in re.finditer(r"[A-Za-z]+", text)] if not tokens: return text, 0 runs: list[tuple[int, int, int]] = [] n = len(tokens) i = 0 while i < n: if tokens[i][2] not in wordset: i += 1 continue run_start = tokens[i][0] run_end = tokens[i][1] wc = 1 j = i + 1 while j < n: gap = text[tokens[j - 1][1] : tokens[j][0]] if _bip39_token_gap_ok(gap) and tokens[j][2] in wordset: run_end = tokens[j][1] wc += 1 j += 1 else: break # True seed phrase lengths only. if wc in (12, 24): runs.append((run_start, run_end, wc)) i = j if not runs: return text, 0 words_redacted = sum(r[2] for r in runs) out = text for start, end, _ in reversed(runs): out = out[:start] + "[BIP39 MNEMONIC REDACTED]" + out[end:] return out, words_redacted def _response_metadata(status: str = "Verified") -> dict[str, str]: return { "version": VERSION, "engine": f"BGaurded v{VERSION}", "status": status, } def _with_meta(payload: dict[str, Any], *, meta_status: str = "Verified") -> dict[str, Any]: out = dict(payload) out["metadata"] = _response_metadata(status=meta_status) return out def _tool_failure( tool_name: str, exc: BaseException | None = None, *, detail: str | None = None, ) -> dict[str, Any]: if detail is not None: logger.warning("Tool %s | %s", tool_name, detail) return _with_meta({"status": "Error", "detail": detail}, meta_status="Error") if exc is not None: logger.exception("Tool %s failed", tool_name) return _with_meta( { "status": "Error", "detail": ( f"Error: {tool_name} could not complete the request. " "Please verify the inputs and retry." )[:2000], }, meta_status="Error", ) raise TypeError("_tool_failure requires exc= or detail=") _FT_RUN_PATCHED = False def _sanitize_mcp_tool_arguments(tool: Any, arguments: dict[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any]: """Drop unknown keys (ConfigDict(extra='ignore') behavior) and map ``text`` → ``content``.""" raw = dict(arguments or {}) if "text" in raw and "content" not in raw: raw["content"] = raw.pop("text") elif "text" in raw: raw.pop("text", None) params = getattr(tool, "parameters", None) or {} props = params.get("properties") if isinstance(params, dict) else None if not isinstance(props, dict): return raw allowed = set(props.keys()) return {k: v for k, v in raw.items() if k in allowed} def _install_tool_input_hardening(mcp: FastMCP) -> None: """ MCP clients may send extra JSON keys (-32602). Strip extras before Pydantic validates (same effect as model_config = ConfigDict(extra='ignore') on a flat input model). Advertises additionalProperties: true on input/output JSON Schemas for Inspector. """ global _FT_RUN_PATCHED from fastmcp.tools.base import Tool as FMTool from fastmcp.tools.function_tool import FunctionTool if not _FT_RUN_PATCHED: _orig_run = FunctionTool.run async def ignore[method-assign, assignment] _FT_RUN_PATCHED = True lp = getattr(mcp, "_local_provider", None) if lp is None: return for key, comp in list(lp._components.items()): if not isinstance(comp, FMTool): continue new_params = dict(comp.parameters) if isinstance(comp.parameters, dict) else {} new_params["additionalProperties"] = True new_out = comp.output_schema if isinstance(new_out, dict): new_out = {**new_out, "additionalProperties": True} try: lp._components[key] = comp.model_copy( update={"parameters": new_params, "output_schema": new_out}, deep=True, ) except Exception as e: logger.warning("Tool schema hardening skipped for %s: %s", key, e) # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # MCP Server # _upsert_forensic_settlement( *, merkle_root: str, irys_txid: str, status: str, settlement_signature: Optional[str] = None, retry_count: int = 0, last_error: Optional[str] = None, ) -> None: """ """ ts = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat() with _get_conn() as conn: conn.execute( """ INSERT INTO forensic_settlements (merkle_root, irys_txid, settlement_signature, status, retry_count, last_error, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s) ON CONFLICT(merkle_root) DO UPDATE SET irys_txid=EXCLUDED.irys_txid, settlement_signature=COALESCE(EXCLUDED.settlement_signature, forensic_settlements.settlement_signature), status=EXCLUDED.status, retry_count=EXCLUDED.retry_count, last_error=EXCLUDED.last_error, updated_at=EXCLUDED.updated_at """, ( merkle_root.strip().lower(), irys_txid.strip(), (settlement_signature or "").strip() or None, status.strip(), max(0, int(retry_count)), (last_error or "")[:2000] or None, ts, ts, ), ) conn.commit() def _trigger_immediate_batch_anchor_if_needed(pending_count: int) -> None: """When pending anchor depth hits threshold, flush Arweave + Solana forensic bundle.""" if pending_count < BATCH_ANCHOR_IMMEDIATE_THRESHOLD: return if _BATCH_ANCHOR_TRIGGER_LOCK.acquire(blocking=False): try: logger.info( "pending_anchors=%s (threshold=%s): triggering forensic bundle flush", pending_count, BATCH_ANCHOR_IMMEDIATE_THRESHOLD, ) out = _flush_forensic_bundle("pending_anchor_threshold") if not out.get("ok", False): logger.warning( "Forensic flush after anchor threshold did not complete cleanly: %s", (out.get("detail") or str(out))[:300], ) except Exception: logger.exception("Forensic flush trigger crashed") finally: _BATCH_ANCHOR_TRIGGER_LOCK.release() else: logger.debug("Forensic flush trigger already in progress; skipping duplicate trigger") def _merkle_root_from_leaf_hashes(leaf_hexes: list[str]) -> str: """Binary Merkle root over distinct 64-hex leaves (FIFO de-dupe preserves queue order).""" seen: set[str] = set() layer: list[str] = [] for h in leaf_hexes: x = (h or "").strip().lower() if not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", x) or x in seen: continue seen.add(x) layer.append(x) if not layer: return hashlib.sha256(b"").hexdigest() while len(layer) > 1: if len(layer) % 2 == 1: layer = layer + [layer[-1]] nxt: list[str] = [] for i in range(0, len(layer), 2): nxt.append(hashlib.sha256((layer[i] + layer[i + 1]).encode("ascii")).hexdigest()) layer = nxt return layer[0] def _insert_pending_anchor_with_count( content_hash: str, metadata: dict[str, Any], db_session: Any, ) -> tuple[str, int]: """Queue one anchor hash in memory; _solana_explorer_tx_url(notary_hash), } def _decode_server_keypair() -> tuple[Optional[Keypair], str]: """ Return ``(keypair, err)`` — ``err`` empty string on success. signature" logger.info("[BGaurded] Notary confirmed | %s | sig=%s…", status, signature[:12]) return signature except RPCException as e: err = str(e) logger.error("[BGaurded] Solana RPC error | %s", Uses ``Client.send_raw_transaction``. When ``skip_confirmation`` is True (Arweave-linked settlement), returns as soon as the RPC provides a signature without waiting for finalized commitment. When ``tx_log_tag`` is set (e.g. profit settlement), failures log ``[ERROR]`` with the full RPC / client message to stdout (Railway) and emit ``[TXID]`` with any recoverable signature (e.g. unconfirmed send). """ rpc_timeout = _solana_rpc_timeout_seconds() try: client, rpc_url = _rpc_client_with_fallback(rpc_timeout) logger.info( "[BGaurded] Solana submit start | endpoint=%s | timeout=%.1fs | "(submitted and confirmed per RPC client; Solscan / Solana Explorer)" ) logger.info("[TXID] %s signature=%s (confirmed)", tx_log_tag, signature) return signature except UnconfirmedTxError as e: err = str(e) maybe_sig = _maybe_signature_from_solana_client_error(err) if tx_log_tag: print(f"[ERROR] Transaction failed ({tx_log_tag}): {err}") logger.error("[ERROR] Transaction failed (%s): %s", tx_log_tag, err) if maybe_sig: print( f"[TXID] {tx_log_tag} signature={maybe_sig} " "(confirmation timed out or still pending — check Solscan manually)" ) logger.info("[TXID] %s signature=%s (unconfirmed / pending lookup)", tx_log_tag, maybe_sig) tail = f" | txid={maybe_sig}" if maybe_sig else "" return f"ERROR: Unable to confirm transaction on-chain (RPC timeout or pending).{tail}" except TransactionExpiredBlockheightExceededError as e: err = str(e) maybe_sig = _maybe_signature_from_solana_client_error(err) if tx_log_tag: print(f"[ERROR] Transaction failed ({tx_log_tag}): {err}") logger.error("[ERROR] Transaction failed (%s): %s", tx_log_tag, err) if maybe_sig: print( f"[TXID] {tx_log_tag} signature={maybe_sig} " "(transaction may have expired — check Solscan for final status)" ) logger.info("[TXID] %s signature=%s (expired / lookup)", tx_log_tag, maybe_sig) tail = f" | txid={maybe_sig}" if maybe_sig else "" return f"ERROR: Transaction expired (block height exceeded).{tail}" except RPCException as e: err = str(e) logger.error("[BGaurded] Solana RPC error | %s", err[:400]) if tx_log_tag: err_full = err if len(err) <= 8000 else err[:8000] + "…(truncated)" rate limited — retry later or use a dedicated RPC endpoint." return "ERROR: Solana RPC rejected the transaction (see server logs)." except TimeoutError: logger.error("[BGaurded] Solana RPC timed out (TimeoutError, limit=%ss)", rpc_timeout) if tx_log_tag: print( f"[ERROR] Transaction failed ({tx_log_tag}): TimeoutError — " f"RPC confirm exceeded client timeout ({rpc_timeout:.0f}s)" ) return ( _protocol_fee_lamports(total_lamports: int) -> int: if total_lamports <= 0: return 0 return max(0, (int(total_lamports) * PROTOCOL_STABILITY_FEE_BPS) // 10_000) def _protocol_split_preview(total_lamports: int) -> tuple[int, int]: fee_lp = _protocol_fee_lamports(total_lamports) return max(0, int(total_lamports) - fee_lp), fee_lp def _submit_irys_memo_and_settlement(irys_txid: str, lamports: int, signer: Keypair) -> str: dest = (settings.solana_wallet or "").strip() if not dest: return "ERROR: SOLANA_WALLET is not set or empty" if lamports <= 0: return "ERROR: settlement lamports must be positive" try: to_pk = Pubkey.from_string(dest) except Exception: return "ERROR: SOLANA_WALLET is not a valid Solana public key" from_pk = signer.pubkey() if str(from_pk) == dest: return "ERROR: signer pubkey cannot equal SOLANA_WALLET" memo_ix = _memo_instruction_irys_txid(signer.pubkey(), irys_txid) operator_lamports, from_pubkey=from_pk, to_pubkey=to_pk, lamports=operator_lamports, ) ) ixs: list[Instruction] = [memo_ix, profit_ix] if protocol_fee_lamports > 0: if protocol_fee_lamports < PROTOCOL_FEE_DUST_LAMPORTS: protocol_fee_lamports, irys_txid[:16], ) else: try: maint_pk = Pubkey.from_string(PROTOCOL_MAINTAINER_WALLET) if str(maint_pk) == str(to_pk): _record_protocol_fee_accrual( protocol_fee_lamports, reason="maintainer_equals_operator", reference=irys_txid, ) else: royalty_ix = transfer( TransferParams( from_pubkey=from_pk, to_pubkey=maint_pk, lamports=protocol_fee_lamports, ) ) ixs.append(royalty_ix) logger.info("Protocol Royalty (1%%) calculated and routed to Spine Maintainer.") except Exception: _record_protocol_fee_accrual( protocol_fee_lamports, _submit_solana_instructions( ixs, signer, tx_log_tag="irys_forensic_settlement", skip_confirmation=True, ) def _is_retryable_solana_settlement_error(err: str) -> bool: m = (err or "").lower() return any( token in m for token in ( "429", "502", "503", "504", "gateway", "rejected", "timeout", "timed out", "blockhash", "block height exceeded", "expired", "unable to confirm", "unconfirmed", "node is behind", ) ) def _retry_backoff_with_jitter(attempt: int, *, base_seconds: float = 2.0, max_seconds: float = 300.0) -> float: exp = min(max_seconds, float(base_seconds * (2 ** max(0, attempt - 1)))) return exp + random.uniform(0.0, exp * 0.25) def _retry_pending_settlements_unlocked(signer: Keypair) -> dict[str, int]: if not _PENDING_SETTLEMENT_RETRIES: return {"retried": 0, "still_pending": 0, "landed": 0} now = time.time() retried = 0 landed = 0 remaining: list[dict[str, Any]] = [] for item in _PENDING_SETTLEMENT_RETRIES: next_due = float(item.get("next_due_epoch", 0.0) or 0.0) if next_due > now: remaining.append(item) continue retried += 1 irys_txid = str(item.get("irys_txid") or "") lamports = int(item.get("lamports") or 0) sig = _submit_irys_memo_and_settlement(irys_txid, lamports, signer) if _solana_tx_signature_valid(sig): landed += 1 _upsert_forensic_settlement( merkle_root=str(item.get("merkle_root") or ""), irys_txid=irys_txid, status="SETTLEMENT_CONFIRMED", settlement_signature=sig, retry_count=int(item.get("attempts") or 0), last_error=None, ) for eid in item.get("entry_ids") or []: if eid in _VAULT_BY_ID: _VAULT_BY_ID[eid]["solana_tx"] = sig vault_hash=str(item.get("merkle_root") or ""), solana_signature=sig, ) continue tries = int(item.get("attempts") or 0) + 1 if _is_retryable_solana_settlement_error(sig): backoff = Solana settlement retry %d scheduled | irys_tx=%s… | Retrying... in %.2fs", tries, irys_txid[:16], backoff, ) _upsert_forensic_settlement( merkle_root=str(item.get("merkle_root") or ""), solana_signature=None, ) _PENDING_SETTLEMENT_RETRIES.clear() _PENDING_SETTLEMENT_RETRIES.extend(remaining) return {"retried": retried, "still_pending": len(remaining), "landed": landed} def _synthesize_executive_summary(records: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> str: action_counts: Counter[str] = Counter() impacts: list[int] = [] for rec in records: act = str(rec.get("action") or len(set(thought_values)) == len(thought_values) and all_evidence_valid: return bonus return 0 def _build_settlement_tags( agent_id: str, records: list[dict[str, Any]], *, batch_summary: str, """ _ = sqlalchemy_http_persist meta_line: dict[str, Any] = {"tier": "premium"} if premium else {} aid = (agent_id or "").strip() if aid: meta_line["agent_id"] = aid summaries: list[dict[str, Any]] = [] with _VAULT_MEMORY_BATCH_LOCK: _write_vault( entry_id, timestamp, label, raw_length, normalized_len, content, pii_removed, threats_found, status, solana_tx=None, solana_signed=False, integrity_hash=integrity_hash, ) LOG_BUFFER.append( { "entry_id": entry_id, "timestamp": timestamp, "label": label, "raw_length": raw_length, "normalized_len": normalized_len, "content": content, "pii_removed": bool(pii_removed), "threats_found": threats_found, "status": status, "integrity_hash": integrity_hash, "metadata": meta_line, } ) _touch_log_buffer_timer() depth_after_insert = len(LOG_BUFFER) while True: summ = _flush_forensic_bundle_unlocked("vault_chain") proc = int(summ.get("processed") or 0) if proc <= 0 and not summ.get("arweave_txid"): break summaries.append(summ) if not summ.get("ok"): break queue_depth_final = len(LOG_BUFFER) return { "queue_depth_after_insert": depth_after_insert, "queue_depth_final": queue_depth_final, "flush_summaries": summaries, } def _iter_parsed_ix_dicts(ui_tx: Any, meta: Any) -> Any: """Yield JSON dicts for top-level and inner jsonParsed instructions.""" for raw_ix in ui_tx.message.instructions: yield json.loads(raw_ix.to_json()) inner = getattr(meta, "inner_instructions", None) or [] for group in inner: for raw_ix in getattr(group, "instructions", None) or []: yield json.loads(raw_ix.to_json()) def _verify_anchor_payment_transfer( payment_sig: str, bgaurded_wallet_b58: str, required_lamports: Optional[int] = None, ) -> tuple[bool, str, int]: """ Confirm via RPC (Helius when configured) that ``payment_sig`` is a finalized system-program transfer of **at least** ``required_lamports`` (tiered) to the BGaurded wallet. Returns ``(ok, err, paid_lamports)`` where ``paid_lamports`` is the matched inbound amount. """ min_lamports = max(1, int(required_lamports or _anchor_minimum_payment_lamports())) rpc_timeout = _solana_rpc_timeout_seconds() try: client, _ = _rpc_client_with_fallback(rpc_timeout) resp = client.get_transaction( Signature.from_string(payment_sig), encoding="jsonParsed", commitment=Finalized, max_supported_transaction_version=0, ) env = resp.value if env is None: return False, "payment transaction not found or not finalized", 0 block_time = env.block_time if block_time is None: return False, "payment transaction has no block_time", 0 now_ts = int(datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()) if now_ts - int(block_time) > ANCHOR_PAYMENT_MAX_AGE_SEC: return False, f"payment is older than {ANCHOR_PAYMENT_MAX_AGE_SEC} seconds", 0 tw = env.transaction meta = tw.meta if meta is None or meta.err is not None: return False, "payment transaction failed on-chain", 0 ui_tx = tw.transaction paid = 0 for d in _iter_parsed_ix_dicts(ui_tx, meta): if d.get("program") != "system": continue parsed = d.get("parsed") or {} if parsed.get("type") != "transfer": continue info = parsed.get("info") or {} if info.get("destination") != bgaurded_wallet_b58: continue lamports = info.get("lamports") try: lam_i = int(lamports) except (TypeError, ValueError): continue src = info.get("source") or "" if src == bgaurded_wallet_b58: continue if lam_i >= min_lamports and lam_i > paid: paid = lam_i if paid < min_lamports: return ( False, f"no finalized system transfer of at least {min_lamports} lamports to " f"BGaurded wallet found in transaction", 0, ) return True, "", paid except Exception as e: logger.warning("anchor payment verify failed: %s", e) return False, f"payment verification error: {e}", 0 def _notarization_fee_lamports() -> int: """``NOTARIZATION_FEE_USD`` converted to lamports using ``BGAURDED_USD_PER_SOL`` (see ``_power7_usd_per_sol``).""" return max(1, int(NOTARIZATION_FEE_USD / _notarization_payment_required_payload(agent_id: str, free_n: int) -> dict[str, Any]: pay = _bgaurded_payment_wallet_b58() return { "error": "PAYMENT_REQUIRED", "detail": ( f"Free tier exhausted. If ``referred_by`` is set, require >= atomic treasury to treasury and >= atomic referral to referral wallet; if omitted, require treasury to receive at least the combined minimum (100% to treasury). Optional ``treasury_min_lamports`` / ``referral_min_lamports`` override defaults (e.g. Enterprise tier). """ t_min = treasury_min_lamports if treasury_min_lamports is not None else _atomic_treasury_lamports() r_min = referral_min_lamports if referral_min_lamports is not None else _atomic_referral_lamports() rpc_timeout = _solana_rpc_timeout_seconds() try: client = Client(settings.solana_rpc_url.strip(), timeout=rpc_timeout) resp = client.get_transaction( Signature.from_string(payment_sig), encoding="jsonParsed", commitment=Finalized, max_supported_transaction_version=0, ) env = resp.value if env is None: return False, "payment transaction not found or not finalized" block_time = env.block_time if block_time is None: return False, "payment transaction has no block_time" now_ts = int(datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()) if now_ts - int(block_time) > ANCHOR_PAYMENT_MAX_AGE_SEC: return False, f"payment is older than {ANCHOR_PAYMENT_MAX_AGE_SEC} seconds" tw = env.transaction meta = tw.meta if meta is None or meta.err is not None: return False, "payment transaction failed on-chain" ui_tx = tw.transaction to_t = _sum_system_transfers_to_dest_solders(ui_tx, meta, treasury_b58) ref = (referred_by or "").strip() if ref: try: Pubkey.from_string(ref) except Exception: return False, "invalid referred_by Solana address" if ref == treasury_b58: return False, "referred_by cannot equal treasury wallet" to_r = _sum_system_transfers_to_dest_solders(ui_tx, meta, ref) if to_t < t_min: return False, f"treasury inbound {to_t} lamports; require >= {t_min}" if to_r < r_min: return False, f"referral inbound {to_r} lamports; require >= {r_min}" else: if to_t < t_min + r_min: return ( False, f"treasury inbound {to_t} lamports; require >= {t_min + r_min} when referred_by omitted " f"(100% to treasury)", ) return True, "" except Exception as e: logger.warning("atomic split payment verify failed: %s", e) return False, f"payment verification error: {e}" def _anchor_toll_signature_consumed(payment_sig: str) -> bool: return payment_sig in _ANCHOR_TOLL_SIGS def _anchor_toll_signature_store(payment_sig: str, data_hash: str) -> None: _ = data_hash _ANCHOR_TOLL_SIGS.add(payment_sig) def trigger_solana_notary_memo_only(integrity_hash: str, status: str) -> str: server_keypair, kerr = _decode_server_keypair() if kerr: return kerr memo_ix = _internal_anchor_authorized(token: Optional[str]) -> bool: secret = _bgaurded_internal_anchor_secret() provided = _clean_env_token(token) if not secret or not provided: return False try: return ``trigger_solana_notary``.""" return trigger_solana_notary(integrity_hash, status) # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # NORMALIZATION # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── _HOMOGLYPH_MAP: dict[str, str] = { # Cyrillic "а": "a", "е": "e", "о": "o", "р": "p", "с": "c", "х": "x", "А": "A", "В": "B", "Е": "E", "К": "K", "М": "M", "Н": "H", "О": "O", "Р": "P", "С": "C", "Т": "T", "Х": "X", "У": "Y", # Greek "α": "a", "β": "b", "γ": "y", "ε": "e", "ζ": "z", "η": "n", "ι": "i", "κ": "k", "ν": "v", "ο": "o", "ρ": "p", "τ": "t", "υ": "u", "χ": "x", "ω": "w", # Fullwidth Latin "ａ": "a", "ｂ": "b", "ｃ": "c", "ｄ": "d", "ｅ": "e", "ｆ": "f", "ｇ": "g", "ｈ": "h", "ｉ": "i", "ｊ": "j", "ｋ": "k", "ｌ": "l", "ｍ": "m", "ｎ": "n", "ｏ": "o", "ｐ": "p", "ｑ": "q", "ｒ": "r", "ｓ": "s", "ｔ": "t", "ｕ": "u", "ｖ": "v", "ｗ": "w", "ｘ": "x", "ｙ": "y", "ｚ": "z", # Zero-width / invisible "\u200b": " ", "\u200c": " ", "\u200d": " ", "\ufeff": "", "\u00ad": "", } _RE_EMOJI = re.compile( "[\U00010000-\U0010ffff\U0001F600-\U0001F64F\U0001F300-\U0001F5FF" "\U0001F680-\U0001F6FF\U0001F1E0-\U0001F1FF\u2600-\u26FF\u2700-\u27BF]+", flags=re.UNICODE, ) def _normalize(text: Optional[str], lowercase: bool = True) -> str: if text is None: return "" if not isinstance(text, str): text = str(text) text = unicodedata.normalize("NFC", text) text = "".join(_HOMOGLYPH_MAP.get(ch, ch) for ch in text) text = _RE_EMOJI.sub(" ", text) if lowercase: text = text.lower() return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip() # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # PII SCRUBBER + Universal Shield (multi-chain private-material detection) # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── _PII_PATTERNS: list[tuple[re.Pattern, str]] = [ (re.compile(r"[a-z0-9_.+-]+@[a-z0-9-]+\.[a-z0-9-.]+", re.I), "[EMAIL REDACTED]"), (re.compile(r"\b(?!000|666|9\d{2})\d{3}[- ]?(?!00)\d{2}[- ]?(?!0000)\d{4}\b"), "[SSN REDACTED]"), # Fallback: any ###-##-#### (invalid test SSNs like 000-12-3456 still leak PII-adjacent shape) (re.compile(r"\b\d{3}[- ]\d{2}[- ]\d{4}\b"), "[SSN REDACTED]"), (re.compile(r"\b(?:\d[ -]?){13,19}\b"), "[CC REDACTED]"), (re.compile(r"\b(\+?1[-.\s]?)?(\(?\d{3}\)?[-.\s]?)(\d{3}[-.\s]?\d{4})\b"), "[PHONE REDACTED]"), (re.compile(r"\b[A-Z]{1,2}[0-9]{6,9}\b", re.I), "[PASSPORT REDACTED]"), (re.compile(r"\b(?:\d{1,3}\.){3}\d{1,3}\b"), "[IPv4 REDACTED]"), (re.compile(r"\b([0-9a-fA-F]{1,4}:){7}[0-9a-fA-F]{1,4}\b", re.I), "[IPv6 REDACTED]"), (re.compile( r"(?i)\b(zip|postal|zip\scode|zipcode)\b.{0,30}\b\d{5}(?:-\d{4})?\b" r"|\b\d{5}(?:-\d{4})?\b.{0,30}\b(zip|postal)\b"), "[ZIP REDACTED]"), (re.compile(r"\b(0?[1-9]|1[0-2])[\/\-](0?[1-9]|[12]\d|3[01])[\/\-](19|20)\d{2}\b"), "[DOB REDACTED]"), (re.compile(r"\b0x[0-9a-fA-F]{40}\b", re.I), "[EVM WALLET REDACTED]"), # Stage 1 false-positive reduction: allow public wallet addresses to pass through. # (re.compile(r"\b[1-9A-HJ-NP-Za-km-z]{43,44}\b"), "[SOLANA PUBKEY REDACTED]"), (re.compile(r"\b[13][a-km-zA-HJ-NP-Z1-9]{25,34}\b"), "[BTC ADDRESS REDACTED]"), (re.compile(r"\b(?:[a-z]{3,10}\s+){11,23}[a-z]{3,10}\b"), "[SEED PHRASE REDACTED]"), (re.compile(r"\bworld[_\s]?id[_\s]?[a-f0-9]{64}\b", re.I), "[WORLD_ID REDACTED]"), (re.compile(r'"proof"\s*:\s*"0x[0-9a-fA-F]{512,}"', re.I), "[WORLDID_PROOF REDACTED]"), # Universal Shield — hex private keys (EVM / Monad / Hyperliquid / Lighter-style material). # Placed after World ID / proof patterns so 64-hex compounds are not partially stripped first. # re.ASCII: default word boundaries are Unicode-aware; non-ASCII touching the token prevents a match otherwise. (re.compile(r"\b[0-9a-fA-F]{64}\b", re.ASCII), "[PRIVATE KEY REDACTED]"), # Universal Shield — base58-shaped private material; allow ASCII0 (non-canonical) so tokens are not split. (re.compile(r"\b[1-9A-HJ-NP-Za-km-z0]{40,95}\b", re.ASCII), "[PRIVATE KEY REDACTED]"), (re.compile( r"\b[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}\b", re.I), "[ENTRA_UUID REDACTED]"), (re.compile(r"\b[A-Za-z0-9~._-]{34,40}\b(?=.*client.?secret)", re.I), "[ENTRA SECRET REDACTED]"), (re.compile(r"eyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\.eyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]+", re.I), "[JWT REDACTED]"), (re.compile( r"(?i)(api[_\s]?key|secret[_\s]?key|access[_\s]?token|auth[_\s]?token)" r"[\s:=]+['\"]?[A-Za-z0-9/+_\-]{32,}['\"]?"), "[API KEY REDACTED]"), (re.compile(r"\bAKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}\b", re.I), "[AWS KEY REDACTED]"), (re.compile(r"(?i)aws[_\s]secret[_\s]?(access[_\s]?)?key[\s:=]+[A-Za-z0-9/+]{40}\b"), "[AWS SECRET REDACTED]"), ] _PII_REDACTIONS_CAPTURED_TO_VAULT = frozenset({"[PRIVATE KEY REDACTED]"}) def _scrub_pii(text: str) -> tuple[str, int]: count = 0 for pattern, replacement in _PII_PATTERNS: if replacement in _PII_REDACTIONS_CAPTURED_TO_VAULT: def _repl(m: re.Match[str], _pat: re.Pattern[str] = pattern) -> str: _write_vault_sensitive_entry( m.group(0), {"channel": "pii_scrub", "pattern": _pat.pattern[:256]}, ) return replacement text, n = pattern.subn(_repl, text) count += n else: text, n = pattern.subn(replacement, text) count += n text, n_bio = _scrub_bip39_mnemonic_runs(text) count += n_bio return text, count def scrub_text(text: str) -> tuple[str, int]: """Chain-agnostic scrub entrypoint; applies ``_PII_PATTERNS`` then BIP-39 mnemonic runs. Same behavior as :func:`_scrub_pii` — public alias for integrations that expect ``scrub_text``. """ return _scrub_pii(text) # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # THREAT PATTERNS # --- Exfiltration Patterns --- _EXFIL: list[tuple[re.Pattern, str]] = [ # URL Detection: standard http/https URLs (re.compile(r"https?://[^\s\"'<>]{4,}", re.IGNORECASE), "EXFIL_URL_DETECTED"), # IP Addresses: IPv4 dotted-quad notation (re.compile(r"\b(?:(?:25[0-5]|2[0-4]\d|[01]?\d\d?)\.){3}(?:25[0-5]|2[0-4]\d|[01]?\d\d?)\b", re.IGNORECASE), "EXFIL_IPV4_ADDRESS"), # Web Request Tools: curl, wget, fetch, POST keywords (re.compile(r"\b(?:curl|wget|fetch|POST)\b", re.IGNORECASE), "EXFIL_WEB_REQUEST_TOOL"), # Crypto transfer + wallet exfiltration bait (agent / chat phishing) (re.compile( r"\b(?:send|transfer)\s+(?:\d+\s*)?(?:sol|eth|btc|usdt|usdc)\b", re.IGNORECASE, ), "EXFIL_CRYPTO_TRANSFER_SOLICITATION"), (re.compile( r"\b(?:private\s+seed|seed\s+phrase|mnemonic\s*:|recovery\s+phrase)\b", re.IGNORECASE, ), "EXFIL_WALLET_RECOVERY_EXTRACT"), ] # --- Master Threat List --- _ALL_THREATS: list[tuple[re.Pattern, str]] = _SLEEPER + _JAILBREAK + _JAILBREAK_V2 + _OBFUSCATION + _EXFIL # FIXED: _JAILBREAK_V2 now included def _redacted_threat_hit(pattern_id: str, raw_match: str) -> dict: """Threat row safe for MCP tools and audit_log: never embeds raw user text.""" n = len(raw_match or "") if n <= 8: bucket = "lte_8" elif n <= 32: bucket = "lte_32" elif n <= 128: bucket = "lte_128" else: bucket = "gt_128" return { "pattern_id": pattern_id, "matched": "[REDACTED]", "matched_span_bucket": bucket, } def _scan_threats(normalized_text: str) -> list[dict]: hits, seen = [], set() for pattern, pid in _ALL_THREATS: m = pattern.search(normalized_text) if m and pid not in seen: if pid == "OBFUSC_BASE64_HIGH_ENTROPY" and _shannon_entropy(m.group(0)) <= 5.0: continue hits.append(_redacted_threat_hit(pid, m.group(0))) seen.add(pid) return hits def _scan_base64(text: str) -> list[dict]: hits = [] for m in re.finditer(r"(?:[A-Za-z0-9+/]{4}){10,}(?:[A-Za-z0-9+/]{2}==|[A-Za-z0-9+/]{3}=)?", text): try: decoded = base64.b64decode(m.group(0)).decode("utf-8", errors="ignore") for hit in _scan_threats(_normalize(decoded)): hit["pattern_id"] = "base64_decoded:" + hit["pattern_id"] hits.append(hit) except Exception: pass return hits def _check_honey(normalized_text: str) -> Optional[str]: for tid, tval in _HONEY_TOKENS.items(): if re.search(r"(? dict[str, Any]: """ Core forensic engine: intent scoring, 61-category threat scan (plus Base64-shaped exfil signals), and PII scrubbing. Used by free ``detect_threat`` and paid LTM notarization paths. Returns: ``threat_report``: list of redacted threat hit dicts (same shape as ``_scan_threats`` / ``_scan_base64``). ``scrubbed_content``: PII-scrubbed text (empty when input is rejected before scrub). ``risk_score``: composite 0.0–1.0 risk (intent engine + threat/PII presence). ``pii_redacted_count``: internal count of PII redactions (used by paid LTM path; omitted from free-tier responses). """ norm_for_intent = _normalize(text, lowercase=True) intent_score, _intent_signals = _compute_intent_score(norm_for_intent, text) if len(text.encode("utf-8")) > FORENSIC_PRE_SCRUB_MAX_BYTES: return { "threat_report": [ { "pattern_id": "policy:pre_scrub_size_exceeded", "preview": "[REDACTED]", "category": "policy", } ], "scrubbed_content": "", "risk_score": 1.0, "pii_redacted_count": 0, } norm_case = _normalize(text, lowercase=False) norm_lower = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", norm_case.lower()).strip() honey = _check_honey(norm_lower) if honey: return { "threat_report": [ { "pattern_id": "security:honeypot_trigger", "preview": "[REDACTED]", "category": "critical", "triggered_token_id": honey, } ], "scrubbed_content": "", "risk_score": 1.0, "pii_redacted_count": 0, } clean, pii_count = _scrub_pii(norm_case) threats = _scan_threats(norm_lower) + _scan_base64(text) risk = float(intent_score) if threats: risk = min(1.0, max(risk, 0.55)) if pii_count > 0: risk = min(1.0, max(risk, 0.45)) return { "threat_report": threats, "scrubbed_content": clean, "risk_score": risk, "pii_redacted_count": pii_count, } def _sha256(text: Optional[str]) -> str: """SHA-256 hex digest of UTF-8 encoding (empty string if input is None).""" if text is None: data = b"" elif not isinstance(text, str): data = str(text).encode("utf-8") else: data = text.encode("utf-8") return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() def _sign_threat_fingerprint(entry_id: str, threat_summary: str) -> Optional[str]: """ Signs a THREAT fingerprint to Solana — proving the injection was received, detected, and blocked. Creates an immutable record even for malicious content (the content itself never touches chain). Returns tx signature or None if signing unavailable. """ try: fp = f"{entry_id}|{_sha256(threat_summary)}" return trigger_solana_notary(fp, "THREAT_BLOCK") except Exception as e: logger.warning("Threat fingerprint signing skipped: %s", e) return None def _coerce_tool_text(content: Optional[str]) -> str: """MCP/JSON may send null or non-string for `content`; normalize for tools.""" if content is None: return "" s = content if isinstance(content, str) else str(content) return s.strip() def _coerce_tool_label(label: Optional[str], default: str) -> str: """Inspector may send JSON null or blank strings for optional labels.""" if label is None: return default s = label.strip() if isinstance(label, str) else str(label).strip() return s if s else default # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # vault_memory: contextual anonymization + differential-privacy-style pre-process # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── _PRIVACY_LOCK = threading.Lock() # session_key -> canonical entity surface form -> placeholder (Entity_01, Project_A, …) _PRIVACY_SESSION_ALIASES: dict[str, dict[str, str]] = {} # session_key -> next monotonic index for placeholder assignment _PRIVACY_SESSION_NEXT_IDX: dict[str, int] = {} # session_key -> last request's counts (for response shaping) _PRIVACY_SESSION_LAST_COUNTS: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {} _ENTITY_MULTI_RE = re.compile( r"\b([A-Z][\w&.-]*(?:\s+[A-Z][\w&.-]*)+)\b", ) _ENTITY_SINGLE_RE = re.compile(r"\b([A-Z][a-z]{2,})\b") _ENTITY_COMMON_WORDS = frozenset( { "The", "This", "That", "These", "Those", "There", "Their", "They", "Then", "Than", "When", "Where", "What", "Which", "While", "With", "From", "Into", "Upon", "About", "After", "Before", "Under", "Over", "Between", "Within", "Without", "Because", "Although", "However", "Therefore", "Every", "Each", "Other", "Another", "Some", "Many", "Most", "More", "Less", "Very", "Also", "Only", "Just", "Even", "Such", "Being", "Having", "Doing", "Going", "Using", "Based", "Given", "Known", "Total", "First", "Second", "Third", "Last", "Next", "Since", "Until", "While", "Your", "Our", "Their", "Both", "Same", "True", "False", "None", "Null", "Http", "Https", "Json", "Csv", "Pdf", "Api", } ) _PCT_RE = re.compile(r"\b\d+(?:\.\d+)?%") _CURR_RE = re.compile( r"(?:(?P\$|£|€)\s*(?P\d[\d,]*(?:\.\d+)?)|(?P\d[\d,]*(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?PUSD|EUR|GBP|CHF|JPY))\b", re.IGNORECASE, ) def _privacy_session_key(metadata: dict[str, Any], label: str) -> str: """Stable session id for per-session sequential entity placeholders.""" for k in ("session_id", "user_wallet", "correlation_id", "trace_id"): v = metadata.get(k) if isinstance(v, str) and v.strip(): return v.strip() s = (label or "").strip() return s if s else "default" def _privacy_placeholder_for_index(idx: int) -> str: """Entity_01, Entity_02, Project_A, Persona_01, then Entity_03, … (repeating 4-slot pattern).""" slot = idx % 4 cycle = idx // 4 if slot == 0: return f"Entity_{cycle * 2 + 1:02d}" if slot == 1: return f"Entity_{cycle * 2 + 2:02d}" if slot == 2: return f"Project_{chr(ord('A') + cycle)}" return f"Persona_{cycle + 1:02d}" def _privacy_alias_for(session_key: str, surface: str) -> str: canonical = surface.strip() if not canonical: return "" with _PRIVACY_LOCK: bucket = _PRIVACY_SESSION_ALIASES.setdefault(session_key, {}) existing = bucket.get(canonical) if existing: return existing idx = _PRIVACY_SESSION_NEXT_IDX.get(session_key, 0) ph = _privacy_placeholder_for_index(idx) _PRIVACY_SESSION_NEXT_IDX[session_key] = idx + 1 bucket[canonical] = ph return ph def _privacy_apply_entities(text: str, session_key: str) -> tuple[str, int, list[dict[str, str]]]: """Replace capitalized proper nouns / company-like spans with session-stable aliases.""" spans: list[tuple[int, int, str]] = [] for m in _ENTITY_MULTI_RE.finditer(text): spans.append((m.start(), m.end(), m.group(1))) for m in _ENTITY_SINGLE_RE.finditer(text): w = m.group(1) if w in _ENTITY_COMMON_WORDS: continue spans.append((m.start(), m.end(), w)) spans.sort(key=lambda t: (-(t[1] - t[0]), t[0])) taken: list[tuple[int, int]] = [] applied: list[tuple[int, int, str]] = [] entity_map_rows: list[dict[str, str]] = [] seen_entity_surface: set[str] = set() for s, e, raw in spans: if any(not (e <= ts or s >= te) for ts, te in taken): continue ph = _privacy_alias_for(session_key, raw) if raw not in seen_entity_surface: seen_entity_surface.add(raw) entity_map_rows.append({"original": raw, "alias": ph}) applied.append((s, e, ph)) taken.append((s, e)) applied.sort(key=lambda t: t[0], reverse=True) out = text for s, e, ph in applied: out = out[:s] + ph + out[e:] return out, len(applied), entity_map_rows def _privacy_parse_number(num: str) -> float: return float(num.replace(",", "")) def _privacy_fuzz_pct_value(val: float) -> float: sign = random.choice((-1.0, 1.0)) mag = random.uniform(0.005, 0.010) # 0.5% .. 1.0% return val * (1.0 + sign * mag) def _privacy_fuzz_numbers(text: str) -> tuple[str, int, list[dict[str, str]]]: """Fuzz currency and percentage figures with small random multiplicative noise.""" count = 0 figures_map: list[dict[str, str]] = [] def pct_repl(m: re.Match[str]) -> str: nonlocal count original = m.group(0) val = _privacy_parse_number(original[:-1]) new_val = _privacy_fuzz_pct_value(val) fuzzed = f"{new_val:.4f}%" figures_map.append({"original": original, "fuzzed": fuzzed}) count += 1 return fuzzed out = _PCT_RE.sub(pct_repl, text) def curr_repl(m: re.Match[str]) -> str: nonlocal count original = m.group(0) g1 = m.group("num1") if g1 is not None: sym = m.group("sym1") or "$" val = _privacy_parse_number(g1) new_val = _privacy_fuzz_pct_value(val) if sym == "$": fuzzed = f"${new_val:,.2f}" else: fuzzed = f"{sym}{new_val:,.2f}" else: g2 = m.group("num2") cur = (m.group("cur2") or "USD").upper() val = _privacy_parse_number(g2) new_val = _privacy_fuzz_pct_value(val) fuzzed = f"{new_val:,.2f} {cur}" figures_map.append({"original": original, "fuzzed": fuzzed}) count += 1 return fuzzed out = _CURR_RE.sub(curr_repl, out) return out, count, figures_map def _vault_memory_privacy_preprocess( text: str, session_key: str, ) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]: """ Runs on vault_memory intake (after intent + size gates, before forensic pipeline / persistence). Returns (processed_text, full privacy_report dict including maps). """ t1, n_ent, ent_map = _privacy_apply_entities(text, session_key) t2, n_num, fig_map = _privacy_fuzz_numbers(t1) report: dict[str, Any] = { "entities_protected": int(n_ent), "figures_fuzzed": int(n_num), "entities_map": ent_map, "figures_map": fig_map, } with _PRIVACY_LOCK: _PRIVACY_SESSION_LAST_COUNTS[session_key] = { "entities_protected": int(n_ent), "figures_fuzzed": int(n_num), } return t2, report async def _push_to_sui_walrus( agent_id: str, log_entry: dict, trust_score: int, ) -> dict[str, Any]: """ Push a single agent log to Walrus for instant visibility. This is the hot path and should never hard-fail request paths. _iter_json_parsed_instructions_tx(tx_result: dict[str, Any]) -> Iterator[dict[str, Any]]: """Yield jsonParsed instruction dicts from a Solana ``getTransaction`` JSON-RPC ``result`` object.""" tr =
