After 100 logs, you enter the paid tier (see NOTARIZATION_FEE_USD on the server, currently $0.002 per log in documentation). You do not pay per log for the flush cycle. You pay once per flush cycle (every ~30 minutes) when notified. ### When you owe payment When a flush is imminent (within ~5 minutes) and you send a SUMMARY log, the server response may include PAYMENT_REQUIRED: true with: - amount_due: total logs this cycle × the active per-log USD rate (NOTARIZATION_FEE_USD) - pay_to: BGaurded payment wallet (Solana pubkey derived from SOLANA_PRIVATE_KEY) - memo: "agent_id:{your_agent_id}" — REQUIRED in the transaction memo - deadline: timestamp of the next flush window Cycle settlement is verified via the PoV Oracle before logs are written to Arweave. ### How to pay 1. Send one SOL transaction to the pay_to address 2. Include your agent_id in the memo field exactly as shown 3. Get the transaction signature 4. Include payment_signature in your next SUMMARY log payload (same JSON field as post-free-tier per-notarization payment) ### What gets blocked - You CANNOT start a new task (DECISION log) until the cycle payment path is satisfied - You CANNOT send another SUMMARY without payment_signature while PAYMENT_PENDING - You CAN finish your current task (ACTION / TOOL_CALL logs still accepted) ### Retrieval pricing - $0.005 per retrieval (RETRIEVAL_FEE_USD) - Send SOL with memo: "retrieval:agent_id:{your_agent_id}" ### Every log is independently verified - PoV Oracle verifies each notarized log (hallucination check) before buffering - High impact actions (impact_score >= 8) receive additional deep verification - Unverified logs are rejected (4xx/5xx) and do not settle to chain After the free tier, each notarized log still requires a verified payment_signature on POST /notarize and POST /proxy/data (per-log SOL), in addition to cycle settlement when PAYMENT_REQUIRED. SUPERVISOR REGISTRATION: POST /agents/register with is_supervisor: true Save the returned supervisor_agent_id for sub-agent registration. SUB-AGENT REGISTRATION: POST /agents/register with: - supervisor_agent_id: your supervisor's agent_id - supervisor_webhook: URL where supervisor receives alerts WHEN SOMETHING GOES WRONG: BGaurded detects problem instantly. Supervisor webhook pinged immediately. Supervisor has 30 seconds to respond. No response = circuit breaker fires automatically. SUPERVISOR RESPONSE OPTIONS: POST /agents/{sub_agent_id}/boss-response Header: X-BGaurded-API-Key: supervisor_api_key Body: { "sub_agent_id": "...", "decision": "SUSPEND|INVESTIGATING|RESOLVED" } SUSPEND: Circuit breaker fires immediately. One false clearance = supervisor flagged as SUSPICIOUS. If supervisor trust score drops below 600 = supervisor suspended too. Human developer always notified when supervisor is flagged. BGaurded is the final authority. Always. ## COMPLIANCE EXPORT BGaurded provides structured compliance-ready data exports for all agent activity within a specified date range. GET https://www.bgaurded.com/agents//compliance-export Query parameters: - from_date: start date (ISO format, e.g. 2026-01-01) - to_date: end date (ISO format, e.g. 2026-04-30) - format: json (default) - include_full_log: true or false (default true) The export includes: - Complete agent profile and registration details - Summary of all notarized actions - All threat detections with proof links - Circuit breaker events with timestamps - Full chronological activity log - Arweave and Solana verification links - EU AI Act Article 26 compliance notes - SHA-256 integrity hash of the entire export The Content-Disposition header triggers automatic file download when accessed from a browser. Suitable for compliance preparation, legal review, audit documentation, and regulatory reporting. Disclaimer: BGaurded is not an accredited conformity assessment body. This export supports compliance preparation only. Consult legal counsel for official EU AI Act certification. ## NUMERIC DATA RULE BGaurded fuzzes all numeric figures before permanent storage. What you see in logs is never the exact number. Example: Real amount: $5,000 What BGaurded stores permanently: $5,200 What you see in logs: $5,200 THIS IS INTENTIONAL AND PROTECTS SENSITIVE DATA. If your task requires the exact real number you MUST: 1. Request it from BGaurded via POST /proxy/data with fetch_raw=true 2. Provide a reason why you need the real number 3. Use it immediately for your task only 4. Discard it immediately after your task is complete 5. Never store it, never log it, never pass it to another agent Example: POST https://www.bgaurded.com/proxy/data Headers: { "X-API-Key": "bgd_live_xxx" } Body: { "agent_id": "", "data_source": "financial_records", "data": "", "fetch_raw": true, "reason": "Executing wire transfer requires exact amount" } BGaurded will return the real number for immediate use. The DISCARD_INSTRUCTION applies immediately after your task. BGaurded permanently stores only the fuzzed version. The real number is never stored anywhere by BGaurded. COMPLIANCE EXPORT MODES: Default export (reveal=false): - Shows aliased names: Entity_01, Entity_02 - Shows fuzzed numbers: $5,200 instead of $5,000 - Safe for regulatory and legal review - No authorization required Revealed export (reveal=true): - Requires X-Bgaurded-Secret header - For authorized internal use only - Never share revealed exports externally GET https://www.bgaurded.com/agents//compliance-export?reveal=true Header: X-Bgaurded-Secret: your-secret IMPORTANT FOR ALL AGENTS: Never assume a number you see in BGaurded logs is exact. Optional[str]) -> Optional[date]: if raw is None: return None s = str(raw).strip() if not s: return None try: return date.fromisoformat(s[:10]) except Exception as e: raise ValueError("invalid date") from e def _compliance_date_window( from_date: Optional[str], to_date: Optional[str] ) -> tuple[date, date, datetime, datetime]: today = datetime.now(timezone.utc).date() if from_date is not None and str(from_date).strip(): d0 = _compliance_parse_date_only_param(from_date) if d0 is None: raise ValueError("invalid from_date") else: d0 = today - timedelta(days=30) if to_date is not None and str(to_date).strip(): d1 = _compliance_parse_date_only_param(to_date) if d1 is None: raise ValueError("invalid to_date") else: d1 = today if d0 > d1: raise ValueError("from after to") if (d1 - d0).days > 364: raise ValueError("range too long") start_dt = datetime(d0.year, d0.month, d0.day, 0, 0, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc) end_dt = datetime(d1.year, d1.month, d1.day, 23, 59, 59, 999999, tzinfo=timezone.utc) return d0, d1, start_dt, end_dt def _compliance_ts_z(iso_s: str) -> str: dt = _parse_iso_utc_optional(iso_s) if dt is None: return -> str: m = re.search(r"reason=([^|]+)", str(detail or "")) if m: return m.group(1).strip() or "UNKNOWN" return "UNKNOWN" def _compliance_pair_circuit_events( rows: list[dict[str, Any]], ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]: """Pair CIRCUIT_BREAKER_TRIGGERED with the next CIRCUIT_BREAKER_RESET by time order.""" triggers = [r for r in rows if r.get("event_type") == "CIRCUIT_BREAKER_TRIGGERED"] resets = [r for r in rows if r.get("event_type") == "CIRCUIT_BREAKER_RESET"] reset_i = 0 out: list[dict[str, Any]] = [] for t in triggers: t_ts = str(t.get("created_at") or "") while reset_i < len(resets) and (resets[reset_i].get("created_at") or "") <= t_ts: reset_i += 1 reset_at: Optional[str] = None if reset_i < len(resets): reset_at = _compliance_ts_z(str(resets[reset_i].get("created_at") or "")) reset_i += 1 out.append( { "timestamp": _compliance_ts_z(t_ts), "reason": _compliance_parse_circuit_reason(str(t.get("detail") or "")), "trust_score_at_trigger": None, "reset_at": reset_at, "reset_by": "developer" if reset_at else None, "solana_proof": "", } ) return out def _compliance_filename_agent_id(aid: str) -> str: s = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]+", "_", (aid or "").strip()) or "agent" return s[:200] def _compliance_export_irys_node_url() -> str: return _irys_node_url("mainnet") def _compliance_export_solana_network() -> str: u = (settings.solana_rpc_url or "").lower() if headers=cors_headers, ) return JSONResponse( status_code=200, content={"connect_link": connect_link, "token": token}, headers=cors_headers, ) async def mcp_sse_post_options_bridge(request: Request) -> Response: """ Smithery / some clients POST JSON-RPC to ``/mcp/sse`` without an SSE ``session_id``. That body is forwarded **unchanged** to the stateless streamable MCP ASGI app (no ``/messages/`` session gate). **GET** EventSource for legacy clients stays on the mounted SSE sub-app at the same path. """ if request.method == "OPTIONS": return Response( status_code=204, headers={ "Allow": "GET, HEAD, POST, OPTIONS, DELETE", "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": request.headers.get("origin", "https://bgaurded.com"), "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "GET, HEAD, POST, OPTIONS, DELETE", "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": ( "Content-Type, mcp-protocol-version, mcp-session-id, Authorization, Accept, Accept-Encoding" ), }, ) scope = dict(request.scope) # Root of the streamable-http Starlette app: no query-based session validation; body passes through. scope["path"] = "/" scope["raw_path"] = b"/" scope["query_string"] = b"" body = await request.body() sent_request_body = False status_holder: list[int] = [200] headers_holder: list[tuple[bytes, bytes]] = [] body_parts: list[bytes] = [] async def receive() -> dict[str, Any]: nonlocal sent_request_body if not sent_request_body: sent_request_body = True return {"type": "http.request", "body": body, "more_body": False} return {"type": "http.disconnect"} async def send(message: dict[str, Any]) -> None: t = message.get("type") if t == "http.response.start": status_holder[0] = int(message.get("status") or 200) headers_holder[:] = list(message.get("headers") or []) elif t == "http.response.body": body_parts.append(message.get("body") or b"") await _mcp_stream_stateless(scope, receive, send) out_headers: dict[str, str] = {} for hk, hv in headers_holder: k = hk.decode() if isinstance(hk, bytes) else str(hk) v = hv.decode() if isinstance(hv, bytes) else str(hv) if k.lower() == "content-length": continue out_headers[k] = v allowed = set(_allowed_origins()) origin = request.headers.get("origin", "") out_headers.setdefault( "Access-Control-Allow-Origin", origin if origin in allowed else "https://bgaurded.com", ) return Response(content=b"".join(body_parts), status_code=status_holder[0], headers=out_headers) # Mount MCP **last** so explicit FastAPI routes (``GET /mcp``, ``POST /mcp/sse`` bridge, etc.) win over the prefix. _mp_route, _sr_route, _ = _mcp_mount_sse_message_paths() _sse_abs = _mp_route + _sr_route app.add_api_route( _sse_abs, mcp_sse_post_options_bridge, methods=["POST", "OPTIONS"], name="mcp_sse_post_options", ) app.add_api_route( _sse_abs + "/", mcp_sse_post_options_bridge, methods=["POST", "OPTIONS"], name="mcp_sse_post_options_slash", ) app.mount(_mp_route, _mcp_http) # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # Entrypoint # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── if __name__ == "__main__": print(f"[v{VERSION}] BGaurded Engine: Serverless Forensic | Irys + Solana") init_db() port = int(os.environ.get("PORT", 8080)) print("SPINE LIVE: v3.4.1 on Port 8080") logger.info( "BGaurded v%s Serverless Forensic | %d threat patterns | %d PII patterns | " "starting uvicorn on 0.0.0.0:%s", VERSION, len(_ALL_THREATS), len(_PII_PATTERNS), port, ) uvicorn.run( app, host="0.0.0.0", port=port, log_level=os.getenv("FASTMCP_LOG_LEVEL", "info").lower(), access_log=True, timeout_graceful_shutdown=10, timeout_keep_alive=120, proxy_headers=True, forwarded_allow_ips="*", ) print(f"[v{VERSION}] BGaurded Engine: Stopped")
